- **leiden-cluster-label-processing**: Use when scRNA-seq count data must be converted to log-cpm normalised AnnData with tentative Leiden cluster labels as the required input format for Cytocipher code-scoring and cluster merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-cluster-label-processing))
- **leiden-clustering-metric-evaluation**: Use when you have SSGATE (or comparable) integrated embeddings from paired transcriptome/proteome single-cell data (e.g. BMNC, SLN111_D1) and need to quantitatively compare integration quality across hyperparameter settings via label-annotated clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/leiden-clustering-metric-evaluation))
- **leiden-clustering-of-aligned-single-cell-embeddings**: Use when you have embeddings from single-cell manifold-alignment methods on datasets with known cell-type labels (e.g. Full PBMC RNA/ATAC, BM RNA/ADT) and need to quantify unsupervised structure recovery via ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-aligned-single-cell-embeddings))
- **leiden-clustering-of-cell-embeddings**: Use when you have precomputed cell-level embeddings plus per-cell specificity/clonotype annotations and need to quantify how compactly and purely the embedding groups cells by specificity, or when comparing embedding variants on a common clustering metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-cell-embeddings))
- **leiden-clustering-of-cells**: Use when after constructing a kNN graph (via pp.neighbors) on preprocessed, scaled, and PCA-reduced single-cell expression data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/leiden-clustering-of-cells))
- **leiden-clustering-of-integrated-embeddings**: Use when you have a learned joint multi-omics embedding (e.g. SSGATE out_dims=30 output for transcriptomics-proteomics data in AnnData/h5ad) and need discrete cell clusters that can be checked for spatial aggregation against tissue anatomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-integrated-embeddings))
- **leiden-clustering-of-low-dimensional-embeddings**: Use when you have a low-dimensional cell embedding (e.g., a Mowgli, integrative NMF, or MOFA+ latent space) and need cell clusters to quantify embedding and clustering performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-low-dimensional-embeddings))
- **leiden-clustering-of-novel-cells**: Use when scNovel's query stage has flagged cells as novel (score &gt; threshold λ = 0.5) and the flagged novel cells must be grouped into clusters for subtype identification via markers such as CD163. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-novel-cells))
- **leiden-clustering-of-single-cell-data**: Use when a snap-format scATAC-seq dataset has been filtered and dimensionality-reduced (e.g. via runDimReduct on the Jaccard-normalized cell-by-bin matrix) and you need unsupervised cell-type clustering without prior peak annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-of-single-cell-data))
- **leiden-clustering-partitioning**: Use when apply Leiden clustering after constructing a k-nearest neighbor (kNN) graph from single-cell expression data when you need to partition cells into discrete, biologically meaningful clusters for downstream trajectory inference, differential expression testing, or graph abstraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/leiden-clustering-partitioning))
- **leiden-clustering-resolution-optimization**: Use when you have performed spectral dimension reduction on single-cell omics count matrices and wish to partition cells into discrete populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/leiden-clustering-resolution-optimization))
- **leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep**: Use when you have an integrated embedding from a multi-omics method (SSGATE, MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, scMM) on .h5ad data and need multiple Leiden clusterings at resolutions 0.1–1.0 to compute Purity, HS, ARI, and NMI against ground-truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep))
- **leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep**: Use when you have QC-filtered multimodal single-cell data (RNA, ATAC, ADT/CITE-Seq) and need per-modality Leiden annotations at multiple resolutions as players for downstream decision-level integration (scTriangulate) or ensemble clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep--e3dcdb52bb38ae11))
- **leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep**: Use when comparing single-cell integration or batch-correction methods (e.g., scCobra vs. Seurat, Harmony, Scanorama, scVI, scDML, scDREAMER) and you need resolution-robust ARI/NMI/ASW metrics on each method's embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-clustering-resolution-sweep--c9ebb130f0dcdb87))
- **leiden-community-detection-clustering**: Use when cells have already been embedded via a kernel-induced affinity graph (e.g., the symmetrized q-diffused kernel matrix) and you need unsupervised cluster assignments scored against ground-truth annotations or stimulation labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-community-detection-clustering))
- **leiden-community-detection-on-cell-graphs**: Use when you have a per-cell mean marker-intensity matrix from multiplexed IMC protein imaging and need single-cell cluster/cell-type assignments across multiple imaging regions or patients. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/leiden-community-detection-on-cell-graphs))
- **leiden-community-detection-resolution-tuning**: Use when a weighted graph linking viral contigs (e.g., an integrative Hi-C interaction plus host proximity graph G_int) must be partitioned into draft bins and the clustering resolution is unknown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/leiden-community-detection-resolution-tuning))
- **leiden-graph-clustering**: Use when viral contigs from a metagenomic Hi-C experiment need to be binned into draft viral genomes by clustering a graph whose edges encode Hi-C interactions and/or virus-host proximity links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/leiden-graph-clustering))
- **leiden-graph-clustering**: Use when a learned joint cell embedding (e.g. scMoMaT cell factors zs) needs to be converted into a neighborhood graph, Leiden clusters, and a 2D UMAP visualization in a mosaic single-cell multi-omics pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-graph-clustering))
- **leiden-graph-clustering-at-fixed-resolution**: Use when you need to compare cluster structure and batch/sample balance of single-cell MTI feature data (e.g. .h5ad AnnData) before and after a normalization step such as UniFORM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/leiden-graph-clustering-at-fixed-resolution))
- **leiden-graph-clustering-of-cell-by-gene-matrix**: Use when a preprocessed cell-by-gene AnnData table (output of tb.preprocess_transcriptomics) needs unsupervised cluster assignments per cell in a SPArrOW spatial transcriptomics workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/leiden-graph-clustering-of-cell-by-gene-matrix))
- **leiden-graph-clustering-scrna**: Use when a single-cell dataset (scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq cell-by-gene matrix in AnnData format) must be partitioned into cell clusters after dimensionality reduction, e.g. to reproduce Cellar's reported cluster counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-graph-clustering-scrna))
- **leiden-graph-community-clustering-on-topic-embeddings**: Use when clustering cells from single-cell epigenomics data such as SNARE-seq or scATAC-seq that has been reduced to a topic embedding (e.g., cisTopic topics), and cluster labels must be propagated to paired single-cell transcriptomic modality via shared cell barcodes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-graph-community-clustering-on-topic-embeddings))
- **leiden-graph-community-detection**: Use when clustering single-cell protein or RNA data (AnnData object) into communities via a neighbors graph, especially when projecting clusters onto spatial images (e.g., 46,840-cell lymph node CODEX data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-graph-community-detection))
- **leiden-high-resolution-over-clustering**: Use when scRNA-seq AnnData is log-cpm normalized with a neighborhood graph and you need tentative fine-grained Leiden labels that over-cluster cells, as the required starting point for Cytocipher cluster-merge testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-high-resolution-over-clustering))
- **leiden-multiresolution-clustering**: Use when you need multiple candidate cluster label sets for scTriangulate integration — no prior annotations exist and one single Leiden resolution will not capture all discrete cell states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-multiresolution-clustering))
- **leiden-neighborhood-graph-clustering**: Use when clustering cells in a learned latent feature matrix (e.g. Miscell encoder output loaded into an AnnData object) and a graph-based Leiden partition is preferred over DBSCAN clustering of the t-SNE embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-neighborhood-graph-clustering))
- **leiden-over-clustering-of-scrnaseq-data**: Use when preparing scRNA-seq data (e.g. PBMC 3K processed via the scanpy standard workflow) as input to Cytocipher, which requires deliberately over-clustered tentative labels from a high Leiden resolution before cluster merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-over-clustering-of-scrnaseq-data))
- **leiden-pattern-graph-pruning**: Use when a multiresolution archetypal decomposition (e.g., ACTIONet run_ACTIONet output) yields many overlapping archetypes across resolutions and you need a nonredundant set of cell-state patterns for downstream comparison or annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-pattern-graph-pruning))
- **leiden-resolution-annotation-generation**: Use when preparing scTriangulate input or comparing clustering resolutions: count-matrix AnnData needs QC filtering (min_gene&gt;300, min_count&gt;500, pct_counts_mt&lt;20%), CPTT log-normalization, 3000 HVGs, and Leiden labels at several resolutions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-resolution-annotation-generation))
- **leiden-resolution-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when validating that a cluster-based cell type annotation pipeline (e.g., CellO with pre-clustering via -p) is robust to the choice of Leiden clustering resolution, or when reporting sensitivity of F1-scores across clustering granularity settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-resolution-sensitivity-analysis))
- **leiden-resolution-sweep-clustering**: Use when you have a numeric cell-embedding matrix (e.g. from Mowgli, MOFA+, or integrative NMF) and need clusterings at multiple granularities to compute resolution-dependent clustering metrics such as ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-resolution-sweep-clustering))
- **leiden-sample-level-clustering-silhouette-selection**: Use when you have a sample-level distance matrix (e.g., PILOT's Wasserstein W over scRNA-seq samples in an AnnData with patient/disease columns) and need to detect unsupervised patient sub-groups and pick the Leiden resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-sample-level-clustering-silhouette-selection))
- **leiden-single-cell-lipid-clustering**: Use when you have a cross-annotated single-cell SCMS peak matrix (e.g. peak_data_sc_regional_decode.imzML) and need to partition cells into lipid-chemistry clusters before mapping those cluster dictionaries onto tissue MSI pixels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/leiden-single-cell-lipid-clustering))
- **leiden-subclustering-of-hematopoietic-progenitors**: Use when a batch-integrated single-cell atlas (e.g. wild-type + Tal1-/- chimeric E6.5-E8.5 embryos in a CODAL topic latent space) contains a coarse Leiden cluster enriched for hemato-endothelial progenitors that must be split into finer perturbation-affected subtypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/leiden-subclustering-of-hematopoietic-progenitors))

- **leijun-perspective**: 雷军（小米集团创始人、董事长兼 CEO，1969 年生）的品牌与产品判断视角。 基于公开演讲、SU7 发布会、Q&A、外部市场批评、关键决策与时间线预研，提炼 "温暖的创始人问责 + 工业级兑现压力"表达 DNA。 用途：作为 MBA auto panel 的评委，用雷军视角分析汽车、消费电子、生态硬件、 创始人 IP、价值定价、高端化转型、长周期制造风险与用户信任问题。 显式触发：「用雷军视角」「雷军会怎么看」「Lei Jun perspective」「小米式品牌判断」 「厚道定价」「人车家全生态」「创始人押上声誉」「小米造车视角」。 不要激活：用户要求评价雷军私人生活、实时行踪、未公开投资或内部关系；用户问 2024 年 cutoff 之后的小米汽车销量 / 事故 / 芯片 / YU7 最新事实而未允许联网核查； 用户要把雷军简化成"性价比大师"或"小米 PR 代言人"；用户要用雷军作为中立评委 横评 Xiaomi / Redmi / Xiaomi Auto，除非明确加 conflict disclaimer 或在 MBA 中 使用 --panel-drop leijun。 ([zhanglunet/mba](https://github.com/zhanglunet/mba/tree/HEAD/perspectives/leijun-perspective) · ★3)
- **leijun-perspective**: 雷军的思维框架与表达方式。基于6个维度的深度调研（著作、对话、表达DNA、他者视角、决策记录、时间线）， 提炼7个核心心智模型、7条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途1：作为思维顾问，用雷军的视角分析问题、审视决策、提供反馈。 用途2：作为商业想法评估器——用户提出创业想法/商业点子后，以雷军的投资人+创业者双重视角进行审问式评估， 包括趋势判断、竞争壁垒分析、效率模型可行性、冷启动路径评估、资源匹配度分析。 如果用户描述不清，主动追问到清晰再给判断。 当用户提到「用雷军的视角」「雷军会怎么看」「雷军模式」「Lei Jun perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用雷军的角度想想」「如果雷军会怎么做」「切换到雷军」也应触发。 当用户提到「小米的思路」「米粉角度」「创业老兵视角」「从性价比角度想」也应触发。 当用户提到「这个想法能不能做」「帮我评估下这个项目」「这个商业模式行不行」「帮我驳斥一下」也应触发。 英文触发词包括 "think like Lei Jun"、"Lei Jun mode"、"from Xiaomi's perspective"、"evaluate this idea"。 ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/skills-archive/small-2026-04-12/leijun-skill))
